        overflow: visible !important;
        text-wrap: balance;

        display: block;
        right: 0;
        left: 0;
        height: var(--overlay-offset, 0.25rem);
        content: "";

      :host(.tooltip-se):after,
      :host(.tooltip-sw):after {
        bottom: 100%

      :host(.tooltip-n):after,
      :host(.tooltip-ne):after,
      :host(.tooltip-nw):after {

        from {

        }
        to {

        }

      :host(:popover-open),

        animation-duration: .1s;
        animation-fill-mode: forwards;

        animation-duration: .1s;

        animation-timing-function: ease-in;
      }

GitHub requires at least one commit (a default branch) before you can create additional branches.</p>
<p>You have two simple options to fix this and create the branch:</p>
<ol>
<li>Quick way via GitHub web UI</li>
</ol>
<ul>
<li>Go to the repository on github.com.</li>
<li>Click Add file → Create new file.</li>
<li>Name the file README.md (or any file) and add one line (for example: "# tesfayattorney").</li>
<li>In the Commit changes area choose "Commit directly to the main branch" OR select "Create a new branch for this commit" and type your branch name (for example: create-branch) and commit.
<ul>
<li>If you commit to main first, afterwards use the Branch dropdown to create your new branch from main.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<ol start="2">
<li>From your local machine (git CLI)</li>
</ol>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Clone the empty repo, add a file, push the initial commit, then create and push the new branch:</p>
<p>git clone <a href="https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney.git" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney.git</a>
cd tesfayattorney
echo "# tesfayattorney" &gt; README.md
git add README.md
git commit -m "chore: initial commit"
git branch -M main
git push -u origin main</p>
<h1>create the branch and push it</h1>
<p>git checkout -b create-branch
git push -u origin create-branch</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>Tell me which branch name you want (I used create-branch as an example). If you’d like, I can create the initial file and branch for you — tell me the file content to add and the exact branch name, and I’ll proceed.</p></div></div><div I can help either way — here’s a short, practical primer plus some options I can prepare for you.</p>
<p>Quick primer (general information, not legal advice)</p>
<ul>
<li>Legal counselling: providing general guidance about legal issues, explaining the law, options, likely outcomes, and next steps. It’s educational and helps clients understand their situation.</li>
<li>Attorney (lawyer): a licensed professional who can give tailored legal advice, represent clients in negotiations and court, draft legal documents, and has professional duties (confidentiality, competence, conflict checks).</li>
<li>Main differences: legal counselling can be informal or educational; only an attorney can give binding legal advice and formally represent you. Counseling may be provided by attorneys or non-lawyers (e.g., legal aid workers), but representation and advice on specific legal strategies should come from a licensed attorney.</li>
<li>When to consult an attorney: when you need personalized advice, representation in court or formal proceedings, contract drafting/review, complex transactions, or when stakes are significant (financial, liberty, property).</li>
<li>Common services attorneys provide: consultations, document drafting and review, negotiations, litigation, mediation/arbitration, regulatory compliance, estate planning, corporate formation, immigration assistance.</li>
<li>Fees and engagement: fee structures include hourly rates, flat fees, contingency fees, and retainers. Ask for a written engagement letter or retainer agreement that explains scope, fees, and billing.</li>
<li>Confidentiality and privilege: communications with an attorney are generally protected by attorney-client privilege (check local rules and exceptions).</li>
<li>How to prepare for a first meeting: gather documents, timeline of events, list of questions, desired outcomes, and any deadlines. Be candid — omissions can affect advice.</li>
<li>Limitations: I’m not a lawyer and can’t give legal advice. If you need binding advice or representation, contact a licensed attorney in your jurisdiction.</li>
</ul>
<p>How I can help next (examples — I can produce any of these)</p>
<ul>
<li>Draft a website/“services” page describing legal counselling and attorney services.</li>
<li>Create a professional README.md for your repo explaining your practice or project.</li>
<li>Draft a client intake form, consultation checklist, or retainer agreement template (general template, not jurisdiction-specific legal advice).</li>
<li>Create a short FAQ about when to seek counselling vs full representation.</li>
<li>Provide links to resources for finding licensed attorneys in your area.</li>
</ul>
<p>Tell me which one you want me to create (pick one): website copy, README.md, intake form, retainer template, FAQ, or something else — and whether you want a brief summary or a longer, ready-to-paste document. Welcome to the repository for Tesfay Attorney. This README contains a concise description of services, how we work, and client-facing information you can reuse on a website, in client packets, or as part of documentation.

<span class="hljs-section" node="[object Object]">## About</span>
Tesfay Attorney provides legal counselling and full attorney representation tailored to each client's needs. We help people understand their options, protect their rights, and pursue the best available outcomes in negotiations, transactions, and litigation.
</code></pre></button></div>

Welcome to the repository for Tesfay Attorney. This README contains a concise description of services, how we work, and client-facing information you can reuse on a website, in client packets, or as part of documentation.

<span class="hljs-section" node="[object Object]">## About</span>
Tesfay Attorney provides legal counselling and full attorney representation tailored to each client's needs. We help people understand their options, protect their rights, and pursue the best available outcomes in negotiations, transactions, and litigation.
</code></pre></button></div>

Welcome to the repository for Tesfay Attorney. This README contains a concise description of services, how we work, and client-facing information you can reuse on a website, in client packets, or as part of documentation.

<span class="hljs-section" node="[object Object]">## About</span>
Tesfay Attorney provides legal counselling and full attorney representation tailored to each client's needs. We help people understand their options, protect their rights, and pursue the best available outcomes in negotiations, transactions, and litigation.
</code></pre></button></div>
<p>Web UI steps to create main and add this README</p>
<ul>
<li>Open <a href="https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney</a></li>
<li>Click "Add file" → "Create new file"</li>
<li>Name the file README.md</li>
<li>Paste the README contents from above into the editor</li>
<li>In the "Commit new file" section choose "Commit directly to the main branch" (GitHub will create main as the default branch when you make the initial commit)</li>
<li>Click "Commit new file"</li>
</ul>
<p>If you’d like me to add this file to the repository for you instead, reply with:</p>
<ul>
<li>Confirm</li>
<li>Any edits you want to the README (for example, real contact info or wording changes)</li>
</ul>
<p>Once you confirm and supply any edits, I’ll proceed.</p></div></div><div class="ChatMessage-module__actions--CS2_w" role="toolbar" aria-label="Message tools" data-testid="nonshared-toolbar"><button Below is what I prepared; follow either the GitHub web UI steps or the git CLI commands to create the initial commit on main.</p>
<p>Web UI (quick, recommended)</p>
<ol>
<li>Open <a href="https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney</a></li>
<li>Click "Add file" → "Create new file"</li>
<li>Name the file README.md</li>
<li>Paste the README contents from the block below into the editor</li>
<li>In the "Commit new file" area choose "Commit directly to the main branch" (GitHub will create main as the default branch when you make the initial commit)</li>
<li>Click "Commit new file"</li>
</ol>
<p>Git (local) commands</p>
<ul>
<li>Clone, add the README, set main, and push:
git clone <a href="https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney.git" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney.git</a>
cd tesfayattorney
<h1>create README.md with the contents below (or paste manually)</h1>
git add README.md
git commit -m "chore: initial commit"
git branch -M main
git push -u origin main</li>

Welcome to the repository for Tesfay Attorney. This README contains a concise description of services, how we work, and client-facing information you can reuse on a website, in client packets, or as part of documentation.

<span class="hljs-section" node="[object Object]">## About</span>
Tesfay Attorney provides legal counselling and full attorney representation tailored to each client's needs. We help people understand their options, protect their rights, and pursue the best available outcomes in negotiations, transactions, and litigation.
</code></pre></button></div>

I can’t push commits from here, and the repository is currently empty (there’s no branch to create a new branch from), so you’ll need to create the initial commit yourself. I prepared the exact README file below so you can paste it and create main quickly.</p>
<p>What I prepared and why: I created a ready-to-paste README.md covering services, process, fees, onboarding, confidentiality, disclaimer, contact placeholders, contribution guidance, and license notes. Once you create the initial commit on main the repository will no longer be empty and I can help create branches or add other files.</p>
<p>Quick, recommended — create main via GitHub web UI</p>
<ol>
<li>Open: <a href="https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney</a></li>
<li>Click "Add file" → "Create new file"</li>
<li>Name the file README.md</li>
<li>Paste the README contents from the block below into the editor</li>
<li>In the "Commit new file" section choose "Commit directly to the main branch"</li>
<li>Click "Commit new file" — GitHub will create main with this initial commit</li>
</ol>
<p>If you prefer Git (local)</p>
<ul>
<li>Run:
git clone <a href="https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney.git" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/Tesfayattorney/tesfayattorney.git</a>
cd tesfayattorney
<h1>create README.md and paste contents</h1>
git add README.md
git commit -m "chore: initial commit"
git branch -M main
git push -u origin main</li>
</ul>
<p>README.md (ready to paste)</p>
<div class="position-relative"><button class="FileBlock-module__container--Dzsqa" aria-label="View file: README.md" data-testid="chat-message-view-file-README.md"><div class="FileBlock-module__header--AdNd3"><div class="FileBlock-module__language--hjnkp"><svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" class="octicon octicon-markdown MarkdownRenderer-module__noFade--P7umO" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16" height="16" fill="currentColor" display="inline-block" overflow="visible" style="vertical-align: text-bottom;"><path d="M14.85 3c.63 0 1.15.52 1.14 1.15v7.7c0 .63-.51 1.15-1.15 1.15H1.15C.52 13 0 12.48 0 11.84V4.15C0 3.52.52 3 1.15 3ZM9 11V5H7L5.5 7 4 5H2v6h2V8l1.5 1.92L7 8v3Zm2.99.5L14.5 8H13V5h-2v3H9.5Z"></path></svg><span class="FileBlock-module__name--PRLLi">README.md</span><span class="VersionName-module__version--FC1cb prc-BranchName-BranchName-jFtg-">v5</span></div><div class="FileBlock-module__expandIconContainer--re33w"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" role="presentation"><g clip-path="url(#clip0_6911_7629)" fill="currentColor"><path d="M14.722 6.047a.75.75 0 11-1.5 0V3.615L10.2 6.638a.75.75 0 01-1.06-1.06l3.023-3.024H9.729a.75.75 0 010-1.499h4.243a.75.75 0 01.75.75v4.242zM1.278 9.953a.75.75 0 111.5 0v2.432L5.8 9.362a.75.75 0 011.06 1.06l-3.023 3.024h2.433a.75.75 0 010 1.499H2.028a.75.75 0 01-.75-.75V9.953z"></path></g><defs><clippath id="clip0_6911_7629"><path fill="#fff" d="M0 0H16V16H0z"></path></clippath></defs></svg></div></div><pre class="FileBlock-module__previewCode--xp45Y FileBlock-module__isClipped--g6E0E" aria-hidden="true"><code><span class="hljs-section" node="[object Object]"># Tesfay Attorney — Legal Counselling &amp; Representation</span>

Welcome to the repository for Tesfay Attorney. This README contains a concise description of services, how we work, and client-facing information you can reuse on a website, in client packets, or as part of documentation.

<span class="hljs-section" node="[object Object]">## About</span>
Tesfay Attorney provides legal counselling and full attorney representation tailored to each client's needs. We help people understand their options, protect their rights, and pursue the best available outcomes in negotiations, transactions, and litigation.
</code></pre></button></div>
<p>What I’ll do next (after you create main)</p>
<ul>
<li>I can create branches, add other files, or make edits to this README on your behalf.</li>
<li>If you want edits now (e.g., real contact info, different wording, or additional templates), tell me what to change and I’ll prepare the updated file.</li>
</ul>
<p>Would you like any edits to the README before you paste it? focus-trap-help-panel" aria-placeholder="Loading..." data-language="markdown" aria-autocomplete="list"><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">#</span><span class="ͼf"> Tesfay Attorney — Legal Counselling &amp; Representation</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">Welcome to the repository for Tesfay Attorney. This README contains a concise description of services, how we work, and client-facing information you can reuse on a website, in client packets, or as part of documentation.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> About</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">Tesfay Attorney provides legal counselling and full attorney representation tailored to each client's needs. We help people understand their options, protect their rights, and pursue the best available outcomes in negotiations, transactions, and litigation.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Services</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Legal counselling and case assessment</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Contract drafting and review</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Representation in negotiations and dispute resolution (mediation/arbitration)</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Litigation and court representation</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Estate planning and wills</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Business formation and compliance</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Immigration assistance</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Regulatory advice and compliance support</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> How we work</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">1.</span> Initial consultation — review facts, documents, and objectives.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">2.</span> Case assessment — explain legal options, likely outcomes, risks, and costs.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">3.</span> Engagement — sign a written engagement/retainer agreement outlining scope, fees, and communication.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">4.</span> Representation — pursue agreed strategy, with regular updates and opportunities for client input.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">5.</span> Closure — resolve matter or transition to follow-up services; deliver final documents and billing summary.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Fees &amp; Billing</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Fee structures may include hourly rates, flat fees, retainers, or contingency arrangements depending on the matter.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> A written engagement letter explains the fee arrangement, billing cycle, and scope of services.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Clients are encouraged to discuss fee estimates and limits during the initial consultation.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Client Onboarding (what to bring)</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Relevant documents (contracts, correspondence, court papers, notices)</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Timeline of events and key dates</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> List of questions and desired outcomes</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Identification and contact information for involved parties</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Confidentiality &amp; Privilege</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">Communications with Tesfay Attorney are treated as confidential and may be protected by attorney-client privilege. Please do not disclose privileged or highly sensitive documents through public channels.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Disclaimer</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">The content in this repository is for informational purposes only and does not constitute legal advice. Use of materials here does not create an attorney-client relationship. For advice specific to your situation, contact a licensed attorney.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Contact</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">To schedule a consultation or ask about services:</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Email: contact@tesfayattorney.example</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Phone: +1-555-555-5555</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Website: https://tesfayattorney.example</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">(Replace the contact details above with your actual contact information.)</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Using this repository</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">This repository can contain website copy, client forms, templates, and other practice materials. If you plan to publish client-facing templates or forms, make sure they are reviewed for compliance with local rules and customized for your jurisdiction.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> Contributing</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">If this repository will be collaborative (team members, paralegals, or contractors):</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Use clear versioning for client-facing templates.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Maintain an approvals workflow before public or client distribution.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼe">-</span> Store confidential or client-specific data outside the public repo.</div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><br></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto"><span class="ͼf ͼe">##</span><span class="ͼf"> License</span></div><div class="cm-line" dir="auto">Specify a license for content reuse (for example, a permissive license or "All rights reserved" for templates that require authorization). If unsure, consult counsel before licensing client-facing materials.</div></div></div></div></div></div><footer class="FilePreview-module__footer--o2xcM"><span aria-live="polite">README.md</span></footer></div></div></div></div></div><script type="application/json" id="__PRIMER_DATA_:r2:__">{"resolvedServerColorMode":"night"}</script></div>
</react-app>


      </main>
